# FJUH hospital drug database: issues found during Notion verification

Discrepancies between the hospital drug information system (10.10.4.25) and the official labels. These were **not** copied into Notion; they are listed here for the drug information team to review.

| Drug (code) | Field | Hospital database says | Official source says | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Meropenem (MER02) | 懷孕分類 | B | The FDA retired letter categories (PLLR). Current label: insufficient human data; no fetal toxicity in animal studies | US label §8.1 |
| Meropenem (MER02) | 哺乳分類 | Excretion in breast milk unknown / use caution | Excreted at very low levels (RID 0.13–0.18%); acceptable during breastfeeding | LactMed NBK501017 (rev. 2021) |
| Meropenem (MER02) | 安定性 | NS: 2 h at 15–25 °C / 18 h at 4 °C; D5W: 1 h / 8 h | Infusion in NS: 1 h at ≤25 °C / 15 h at ≤5 °C; in D5W: use immediately. Bolus vial in SWFI: 3 h / 13 h | US label §2 (DailyMed 2026) |
| Meropenem (MER02) | 藥動學 | "CSF concentrations approximate those of the plasma" | CSF 1.1–3.3 mg/L (inflamed meninges, pediatric) vs plasma peak ~49 mg/L after 1 g | US label §12.3 |
| Colistin (COL04) | 腎功能調整 (severe row) | "1.5 mg/kg/day OR 100 mg/day, every 36 hours" | **1.5 mg/kg every 36 hours** (≈1 mg/kg/day), CrCl 10–29 | US label (Coly-Mycin M), Dosage table |
| Colistin (COL04) | 腎功能調整 (basis) | Bands by serum creatinine (old US label) | Current US label uses CrCl (≥80 / 50–79 / 30–49 / 10–29) | US label (DailyMed 2026) |
| Colistin (COL04) | 規格 | "Sodium Colistin Methanesulfonate 66.6 mg (2 MIU)/Vial" | 2 MIU ≈ 66.8 mg colistin base activity (CBA) ≈ 160 mg CMS. 66.6 vs 66.8 also inconsistent | UK SmPC conversion table |
| Colistin (COL04) | 警語 / 副作用 | No mention of pseudo-Bartter syndrome | Renal tubulopathy with hypokalemia, metabolic alkalosis, hypocalcemia, hypomagnesemia; monitor electrolytes | US label (Warnings); UK SmPC 4.4 |
| Colistin (COL04) | 哺乳分類 | Avoided | Minimal milk excretion, poor oral absorption; inhaled use acceptable | LactMed NBK501329 (rev. 2024) |
| Colistin (COL04) | 懷孕分類 | C | FDA letter categories retired; label: crosses placenta, use only if benefit justifies risk | US label |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 腎功能調整 | CrCl 60–<130: no adjustment; 30–60: 250 mg q6h or 500 mg q8h; 15–30: 250 mg q8h or 500 mg q12h | CrCl 60–<90 **requires reduction** (400 mg q6h, or 500 mg q6h / 750 mg q8h); 30–<60: 300 mg q6h or 500 mg q8h; 15–<30: 200 mg q6h or 500 mg q12h | US label Table 3 (Primaxin, DailyMed 2025) |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 腎功能調整 | No guidance for CrCl <15 or HD | CrCl <15: do not give unless HD within 48 h; HD: use the 15–<30 dose, give after HD | US label §2.3–2.4 |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 副作用 | Lists rash, GI, local reactions, leukopenia only | **Seizures**, myoclonus, confusion (↑ with renal impairment, CNS disease); not for meningitis or pediatric CNS infections | US label §1.9, §5.2 |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 交互作用 | No interaction field | Valproic acid/divalproex (generally not recommended), ganciclovir (seizures), probenecid (not recommended) | US label §7 |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 劑量 | No maximum stated | Max 4 g/day | US label §2.1 |
| Imipenem/Cilastatin (CUL01) | 哺乳分類 | Manufacturer: weigh risk/benefit | Low milk levels; acceptable in nursing mothers | LactMed NBK500987 (rev. 2024) |

| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Pregnancy (VFE01, VFE02 禁忌症 item 5 and 懷孕分類) | Pregnancy is listed as a contraindication (禁忌症 item 5) and given 懷孕分類 D | No label lists pregnancy as a contraindication. TW/UK: do not use unless the benefit to the mother clearly outweighs the fetal risk; effective contraception. US: can cause fetal harm, no letter category (FDA retired the letter categories). | US label 4, 5.9, 8.1; UK SmPC 4.3/4.6 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/7976/smpc; TW insert 4 禁忌 and 懷孕 https://mcp.fda.gov.tw/im_detail_1/%E8%A1%9B%E7%BD%B2%E8%97%A5%E8%BC%B8%E5%AD%97%E7%AC%AC023648%E8%99%9F |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Contraindications – hypersensitivity (VFE01, VFE02 item 1) | Hypersensitivity to voriconazole 'or other azoles' is a contraindication | The contraindication covers voriconazole or its excipients only. US: no information on cross-sensitivity with other azoles. UK/TW: use caution in patients hypersensitive to other azoles; this is a warning, not a contraindication. | US label 4; UK SmPC 4.3/4.4 Hypersensitivity; TW insert 4 禁忌 / 5.1 過敏 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Contraindications – CYP3A4 substrate list (VFE01, VFE02 items 2 and 4) | Lists terfenadine, astemizole, cisapride, pimozide, quinidine, ivabradine, sirolimus, efavirenz, rifabutin, tolvaptan (VFE01 only) and ergot alkaloids | Current US/UK/TW contraindications also include lurasidone, naloxegol, finerenone, eplerenone, voclosporin, and venetoclax (initiation/ramp-up), and St John's wort among the inducers. Tolvaptan is contraindicated in every label but is missing from the VFE02 list. | US label 4; UK SmPC 4.3; TW insert 4 禁忌 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Contraindications – efavirenz and ritonavir (VFE01, VFE02 item 3) | Lists 'ritonavir, efavirenz' as contraindicated with no dose qualifier | Only efavirenz ≥400 mg once daily and high-dose ritonavir (400 mg q12h or more) are contraindicated. Efavirenz 300 mg/day can be given with voriconazole 400 mg PO q12h. Low-dose ritonavir should be avoided unless benefit/risk justifies it. | US label 4 and Table 10; UK SmPC 4.3/4.4; TW insert 4 禁忌 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Breastfeeding (VFE01, VFE02 哺乳分類) | 'avoided' | The official sources disagree. TW and UK labels require stopping breastfeeding when treatment starts. LactMed says voriconazole is not a reason to discontinue breastfeeding, though an alternative may be preferred for a newborn or preterm infant. The US label says to weigh the benefits. The hospital entry is consistent with TW/UK but does not mention the LactMed view. | TW insert 哺乳; UK SmPC 4.6; LactMed NBK575380 https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK575380/; US label 8.2 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Aspergillosis duration (VFE01 劑量和給藥方法) | 'duration ≥6 months' | No label sets a minimum of 6 months. UK: treatment as short as possible; exposure beyond 180 days needs a benefit-risk assessment. IDSA 2016: treat IPA for a minimum of 6–12 weeks. | UK SmPC 4.2 Duration of treatment / 4.4 Long-term treatment; IDSA 2016 aspergillosis guideline https://www.idsociety.org/practice-guideline/aspergillosis/ (PMID 27365388) |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Renal (VFE01 腎功能調整劑量) | CrCl <50 and haemodialysis: 'No dosage adjustment necessary; however, use of oral voriconazole or alternative antifungals is preferred'. CRRT/PIRRT: oral or an alternative is preferred. | Broadly consistent with the labels. However, the labels say IV should be used only when an assessment of benefit/risk justifies it, because the SBECD vehicle accumulates, and that serum creatinine must be closely monitored, with a switch to oral if it rises. The hospital text leaves out the SCr monitoring and the reason. Labels say nothing about CRRT. This is a minor wording gap, not a contradiction. | TW insert 3.3 腎功能不全; US label 2.6; UK SmPC 4.2 Renal impairment |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Pharmacokinetics (VFE02 藥動學) | 'The elimination half-life is about 6 hours' | Because of non-linear pharmacokinetics, the terminal half-life is dose-dependent and is not useful for predicting accumulation or elimination. | US label 12.3 Pharmacokinetics https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=ce3ef5cf-3087-4d92-9d94-9eb8287228db |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Pregnancy (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | 懷孕分類 D; contraindication list item '5. Pregnancy' | Letter categories have been retired (PLLR). No label lists pregnancy as a contraindication. US: can cause fetal harm, inform the patient of the hazard. TW/UK: do not use unless the benefit to the mother clearly outweighs the risk to the fetus, and use effective contraception. | US FDA label §4, §8.1 https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=ce3ef5cf-3087-4d92-9d94-9eb8287228db; UK SmPC §4.3, §4.6 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/7976/smpc; Taiwan 仿單 §4, §6.1 https://mcp.fda.gov.tw/im_detail_1/%E8%A1%9B%E7%BD%B2%E8%97%A5%E8%BC%B8%E5%AD%97%E7%AC%AC023648%E8%99%9F |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Breastfeeding (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | 哺乳分類: avoided | This is consistent with Taiwan 仿單 §6.2 AND UK SmPC §4.6, which both say stop breastfeeding at the start of treatment. The US label §8.2 says weigh benefit against risk, and LactMed says it is not a reason to stop breastfeeding but an alternative may be preferred. The official sources genuinely differ, so this is not purely a hospital error. Unlike the brief, I found that UK also requires stopping. | Taiwan 仿單 §6.2; UK SmPC §4.6 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/7976/smpc; US §8.2; LactMed NBK575380 https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK575380/ |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Contraindications: hypersensitivity (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | 'Hypersensitivity to voriconazole or other azoles' | Only hypersensitivity to voriconazole or its excipients is a contraindication. For other azoles, UK §4.4 and TW §5.1 say use caution, and the US label says there is no information on cross-sensitivity. The hospital turns a precaution into a contraindication. | US FDA label §4; UK SmPC §4.3/§4.4; Taiwan 仿單 §4/§5.1 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Contraindications: interacting drugs (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Lists terfenadine, astemizole, cisapride, pimozide, quinidine, ivabradine; rifampin, carbamazepine, long-acting barbiturates, ritonavir, efavirenz, rifabutin; sirolimus, efavirenz, rifabutin, ergot alkaloids (tolvaptan appears only on VFE01). | The list is missing St John's wort, lurasidone, naloxegol, eplerenone, finerenone, voclosporin and venetoclax (initiation/ramp-up), and tolvaptan on the tablet records. Efavirenz and ritonavir are contraindicated only at efavirenz ≥400 mg/day and ritonavir ≥400 mg q12h. Low-dose ritonavir should be avoided, and efavirenz 300 mg/day can be used with voriconazole 400 mg q12h. Rifabutin is contraindicated in the US and TW but only 'avoid' in the UK. | US FDA label §4 and §7; UK SmPC §4.3; Taiwan 仿單 §4 https://mcp.fda.gov.tw/im_detail_1/%E8%A1%9B%E7%BD%B2%E8%97%A5%E8%BC%B8%E5%AD%97%E7%AC%AC023648%E8%99%9F |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Renal dose (VFE01, IV) | For CrCl <50, the brief records 'no dosage adjustment necessary' with oral voriconazole or alternative antifungals preferred. The scraped page text lost the '<50' clause, so I confirmed the 'preferred' wording but not 'no dosage adjustment'. For CRRT/PIRRT, oral or an alternative is preferred. | Taiwan and UK: SBECD accumulates, so give oral unless the benefit/risk assessment justifies IV, and monitor SCr closely. US: avoid IV when CrCl <50. All three labels say a dosing change is needed, so 'no adjustment' understates this. CRRT is not in any label. Kiser 2015 (PMID 25645660) found CVVH clears SBECD and standard IV doses can be used. | Taiwan 仿單 §3.3; UK SmPC (IV) §4.2; US §2.6; Kiser 2015 https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25645660/ |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Adult dose: aspergillosis duration (VFE01) | '6 mg/kg twice daily for 2 doses, then 4 mg/kg twice daily; duration ≥6 months' | No label gives a minimum of 6 months. UK §4.4 says IV treatment should be no longer than 6 months, and exposure over 180 days needs a benefit–risk assessment. IDSA 2016 recommends a minimum of 6–12 weeks. | UK SmPC §4.2/§4.4 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/7976/smpc; IDSA Aspergillosis 2016 https://www.idsociety.org/practice-guideline/aspergillosis/ |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Adult dose: candidemia IV fixed dose (VFE01) | 'Initial 400 mg IV twice daily for 2 doses, then 200 to 300 mg IV or orally twice daily' | Label IV dosing is weight-based: 6 mg/kg q12h × 2, then 3–4 mg/kg q12h. The fixed 400 mg and 200–300 mg doses are oral regimens in the labels (UK/TW). IDSA 2016 gives '400 mg (6 mg/kg) ... then 200 mg (3 mg/kg)' without naming a route. | US §2.3 Table 1; UK SmPC §4.2; IDSA Candidiasis 2016 https://www.idsociety.org/practice-guideline/candidiasis/ |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Adult dose: oral loading (VFE02, VOR03) | Aspergillosis: 200 mg twice daily (may increase to 300 mg per TDM); no loading dose stated | UK and TW: start oral therapy with a 400 mg q12h loading dose for the first 24 h (200 mg q12h if <40 kg). The US label uses IV loading for IA and keeps IV going for at least 7 days before switching. | UK SmPC (tablet) §4.2 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/8408/smpc; Taiwan 仿單 §3.1; US §2.3 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Indications / 臨床用途 (all records) | Invasive aspergillosis, serious invasive Candida infection, etc. Prophylaxis is not listed. | UK §4.1 lists prophylaxis of invasive fungal infection in high-risk allogeneic HSCT recipients, and TW §2 lists prophylaxis in high-risk patients including HSCT. Prophylaxis therefore counts as approved under the database rule (FDA or UK). | UK SmPC §4.1 https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/7976/smpc; Taiwan 仿單 §2 |
| Vfend (Voriconazole) (VFE01, VFE02, VOR03) | Product status (VOR03) | Generic voriconazole 200 mg tab marked 臨採 (temporary purchase) | Not a label issue. The pharmacist should confirm whether VOR03 is still in use before it is referenced. | pharmacy.fjuh.fju.edu.tw Key_Search/P4?query=VOR03 (identification only) |
| Sulampi (Ampicillin/Sulbactam) (SUL05) | 適應症 (Indications) | Infections caused by ... Anaerobic bacteria (Bacteroides Fusobacterium, Clostridium difficile), and Branhumella catarrhalis | US label lists 'Clostridium species' (non-β-lactamase producers) and names C. difficile only as the cause of CDAD (WARNINGS). The C. difficile text is copied from the old Taiwan insert's indication paragraph (confirmed on the PDF image) and is clinically wrong. 'Branhumella' is misspelled in the Taiwan insert itself; the correct genus is Branhamella, now Moraxella catarrhalis (US label MICROBIOLOGY). | DailyMed UNASYN MICROBIOLOGY–Anaerobes, WARNINGS–CDAD https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=155c7ec0-5862-404f-b1d0-f278f8a8bbda; Taiwan insert 【適應症】 https://www.cth.org.tw/public/medi_news/9e24641f15ab702170213ee1b96e6469.pdf |

| Sulampi (Ampicillin/Sulbactam) (SUL05) | 哺乳分類 (Lactation) | The manufacturer recommends that caution be used if administering to breastfeeding patients. | This matches the US label 'caution', but LactMed (designated source) says the drug is acceptable in nursing mothers. | Sulampi (Ampicillin/Sulbactam) (SUL05) | 腎功能調整劑量 (Renal) | CrCl [≥]30 mL/minute: No dosage adjustment necessary; 15–29: q12h; 5–14: q24h (the '≥' sign is lost in the page HTML) | US label: CrCl ≥30 → 1.5–3 g every 6 to 8 hours. | Sulampi (Ampicillin/Sulbactam) (SUL05) | Route (成人/小兒劑量) | Adult 'IM, IV: 1.5 to 3 g every 6 hours'; pediatric mild-moderate 'IV, IM' | The Sulampi insert describes an IV-only product: '靜脈乾粉注射劑 ... 可以用靜脈內注射的途徑給予'. The Menocik Taiwan insert §4 lists only tetracycline hypersensitivity. Hepatic dysfunction is 'use with caution'. 'Complete renal failure' and 'children <12 y' are contraindications only in the UK acne MR SmPC 4.3. Myasthenia gravis is only a caution in UK SmPC 4.4 ('Caution is advised... weak neuromuscular blockade'). This is minor, and I took the hospital values from the source brief without opening the hospital page myself. | US FDA D&A ; UK SmPC 4.2/4.4 ; Taiwan 仿單 3.1 ; Rybak 2020 PMID 32191793 https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32191793/ |

| Bobimixyn (Polymyxin B) (BOB01) | Renal dose (腎功能調整劑量) | No dosage adjustment is necessary for any degree of kidney dysfunction | The Taiwan insert for this product gives no adjustment only for CrCl 60–89. The hospital's wording follows the 2019 consensus (R17), not the label. | TW insert 3.3, 6.7 — https://mcp.fda.gov.tw/im_detail_1/%E8%A1%9B%E9%83%A8%E8%97%A5%E8%A3%BD%E5%AD%97%E7%AC%AC061557%E8%99%9F; US label (Adults and children) — https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=b56f18c0-ef5e-4ed9-a5af-f79f3cd189b6 |
| Bobimixyn (Polymyxin B) (BOB01) | HD dosing | Administer one of the twice-daily doses after dialysis on dialysis days | Neither label has an HD dosing instruction. | Zirocin (Azithromycin) (ZIR01, AZI01) | Renal dosing (ZIR01 and AZI01) | ZIR01: "Mild to severe impairment: No dosage adjustment necessary"; HD, PD and CRRT need no adjustment or supplement. AZI01: "No dosage adjustment necessary". | GFR 10–80: no adjustment. GFR <10: use with caution, because AUC rises about 30–35% and Cmax about 60% (Zirocin 仿單 §6.7; Azithrom 仿單 §3.3/§6.7; US FDA 12.3; UK SmPC 4.2). No label has dialysis data; UK SmPC 5.2 says dialysis is unlikely to remove the drug. | Flusine (Flucytosine) (FLU07) | 交互作用 (no field / not mentioned) | No drug interactions listed | TW: brivudine and sorivudine must not be co-administered; wait at least 4 weeks after stopping them before starting flucytosine, and wait 24 h after the last flucytosine dose before starting them. US: cytarabine inactivates flucytosine. Drugs that impair glomerular filtration prolong its half-life. Bone-marrow-depressing drugs or radiation raise marrow toxicity. | TW 仿單 §7.1; US label Drug Interactions / WARNINGS |
| Flusine (Flucytosine) (FLU07) | 副作用 / 警語 | Nausea or vomiting in high dose, diarrhea, elevated LFTs, skin rashes, bone marrow suppression | The list leaves out the US boxed warning (extreme caution in renal impairment; close hematologic, renal and hepatic monitoring). It also leaves out hepatic necrosis (can be fatal), aplastic anemia, agranulocytosis and fatal bone-marrow aplasia, renal failure and crystalluria, CNS effects (confusion, hallucinations, convulsions), hearing loss, peripheral neuropathy, cardiac toxicity, hypoglycemia and hypokalemia, and Lyell's syndrome. It says nothing about serum-level monitoring (toxicity above 100 mcg/mL). | US label boxed WARNING / ADVERSE REACTIONS / OVERDOSAGE https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=01d874f9-e073-4092-94ad-da361551d4bf |
| Flusine (Flucytosine) (FLU07) | 適應症 | Treatment of susceptible fungal infections caused by Candida or Cryptococcus | This matches the US label. The TW insert for Flusine, however, lists 白色黴菌病、黴菌性肺炎及產色黴菌病 (candidiasis, fungal pneumonia, chromomycosis) and does not name Cryptococcus. The hospital entry leaves out chromomycosis and does not say which label it follows. | TW 仿單 §2; US label INDICATIONS AND USAGE |
| Flusine (Flucytosine) (FLU07) | 小兒調整劑量 | (blank) | US label: not systematically studied in pediatric patients. Neonates have been treated with 25–200 mg/kg/day. TW: no information. | US label Pediatric Use / Pharmacokinetics in Pediatric Patients; TW 仿單 §6.4 |
| Flusine (Flucytosine) (FLU07) | 哺乳分類 | Excretion in breast milk unknown/ not recommended. | The US label does not say 'not recommended'. This is a minor wording difference. | US label Nursing Mothers; TW 仿單 §6.2 |
